## 📦 Thorn: ADMAnalysis<a id='admanalysis'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **ADMAnalysis::normalize\_dtheta\_dphi** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Project angular components onto r*dtheta and r*sin(theta)*dphi? <br> *Tartomány:* `Project angular components onto r*dtheta and r*sin(theta)*dphi?` |
| **ADMAnalysis::ricci\_persist** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Keep storage of  the  Ricci tensor and scalar around? <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **ADMAnalysis::ricci\_prolongation\_type** | `KEYWORD` | `none` | The kind of boundary prolongation for the Ricci tensor and scalar <br> *Tartomány:* `no prolongation (use this if you do not have enough time levels active)` |
| **ADMAnalysis::ricci\_timelevels** | `INT` | `1` | Number of time levels for the Ricci tensor and scalar <br> *Tartomány:* `1:3` |

## 📦 Thorn: ADMBaseX<a id='admbasex'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **ADMBaseX::initial\_data** | `KEYWORD` | `Cartesian Minkowski` | Initial metric and extrinsic curvature datasets <br> *Tartomány:* `linear wave` |
| **ADMBaseX::initial\_dtlapse** | `KEYWORD` | `zero` | Initial dtlapse value <br> *Tartomány:* `Dtlapse is zero` |
| **ADMBaseX::initial\_dtshift** | `KEYWORD` | `zero` | Initial dtshift value <br> *Tartomány:* `Dtshift is zero` |
| **ADMBaseX::initial\_lapse** | `KEYWORD` | `one` | Initial lapse value <br> *Tartomány:* `Uniform lapse` |
| **ADMBaseX::initial\_shift** | `KEYWORD` | `zero` | Initial shift value <br> *Tartomány:* `Shift is zero` |
| **ADMBaseX::linear\_wave\_amplitude** | `REAL` | `1.0e-8` | Linear wave amplitude <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:*` |
| **ADMBaseX::linear\_wave\_wavelength** | `REAL` | `1.0` | Linear wave wavelength <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:*` |
| **ADMBaseX::noise\_amplitude** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Noise amplitude <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:*` |

## 📦 Thorn: ADMBase<a id='admbase'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **ADMBase::admbase\_boundary\_condition** | `STRING` | `flat` | Boundary condition for ADMBase variables <br> *Tartomány:* `,` |
| **ADMBase::dtlapse\_evolution\_method** | `KEYWORD` | `static` | The dtlapse evolution method <br> *Tartomány:* `dtlapse is not evolved and initial data is used to fill in new grid points before each step and after grid changes` |
| **ADMBase::dtshift\_evolution\_method** | `KEYWORD` | `static` | The dtshift evolution method <br> *Tartomány:* `dtshift is not evolved and initial data is used to fill in new grid points before each step and after grid changes` |
| **ADMBase::evolution\_method** | `KEYWORD` | `static` | The metric an extrinsic curvature evolution method <br> *Tartomány:* `The metric and extrinsic curvature are not evolved and initial data is used to fill in new grid points before each step and after grid changes` |
| **ADMBase::initial\_data** | `KEYWORD` | `Cartesian Minkowski` | Initial metric and extrinsic curvature datasets <br> *Tartomány:* `Minkowski values in cartesian coordinates` |
| **ADMBase::initial\_dtlapse** | `KEYWORD` | `none` | Initial dtlapse value <br> *Tartomány:* `Dtlapse is zero` |
| **ADMBase::initial\_dtshift** | `KEYWORD` | `none` | Initial dtshift value <br> *Tartomány:* `Dtshift is zero` |
| **ADMBase::initial\_lapse** | `KEYWORD` | `one` | Initial lapse value <br> *Tartomány:* `Uniform lapse` |
| **ADMBase::initial\_shift** | `KEYWORD` | `zero` | Initial shift value <br> *Tartomány:* `Shift is zero` |
| **ADMBase::lapse\_evolution\_method** | `KEYWORD` | `static` | The lapse evolution method <br> *Tartomány:* `lapse is not evolved and initial data is used to fill in new grid points before each step and after grid changes` |
| **ADMBase::lapse\_prolongation\_type** | `KEYWORD` | `Lagrange` | The kind of boundary prolongation for the lapse <br> *Tartomány:* `no prolongation (use this if you do not have enough time levels active)` |
| **ADMBase::lapse\_timelevels** | `INT` | `1` | Number of time levels for the lapse <br> *Tartomány:* `0:3` |
| **ADMBase::metric\_prolongation\_type** | `KEYWORD` | `Lagrange` | The kind of boundary prolongation for the metric and extrinsic curvature <br> *Tartomány:* `no prolongation (use this if you do not have enough time levels active)` |
| **ADMBase::metric\_timelevels** | `INT` | `1` | Number of time levels for the metric and extrinsic curvature <br> *Tartomány:* `0:3` |
| **ADMBase::metric\_type** | `KEYWORD` | `physical` | The semantics of the metric variables (physical, static conformal, etc) <br> *Tartomány:* `metric and extrinsic curvature are the physical ones` |
| **ADMBase::shift\_evolution\_method** | `KEYWORD` | `static` | The shift evolution method <br> *Tartomány:* `shift is not evolved and initial data is used to fill in new grid points before each step and after grid changes` |
| **ADMBase::shift\_prolongation\_type** | `KEYWORD` | `Lagrange` | The kind of boundary prolongation for the shift <br> *Tartomány:* `no prolongation (use this if you do not have enough time levels active)` |
| **ADMBase::shift\_timelevels** | `INT` | `1` | Number of time levels for the shift <br> *Tartomány:* `0:3` |

## 📦 Thorn: ADMDerivatives<a id='admderivatives'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **ADMDerivatives::spatial\_deriv\_order** | `INT` | `-1` | Order of spatial derivatives <br> *Tartomány:* `2:8` |
| **ADMDerivatives::store\_cartesian\_derivatives** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Switch calculation of cartesian derivatives of ADM lapse, shift, 3-metric on/off <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **ADMDerivatives::store\_radial\_derivatives** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Switch calculation of radial derivatives of ADM lapse, shift, 3-metric on/off <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **ADMDerivatives::store\_time\_derivatives** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Switch calculation of time derivatives of ADM lapse, shift, 3-metric on/off <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **ADMDerivatives::synchronize** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | switch off synchronization -- experimental <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **ADMDerivatives::timelevels** | `INT` | `1` | Number of timelevels for all variables <br> *Tartomány:* `Useful for time interpolation` |

## 📦 Thorn: ADMMass<a id='admmass'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **ADMMass::ADMMass\_Debug** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Enable some info at runtime <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **ADMMass::ADMMass\_Excise\_Horizons** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Should we exclude the region inside the AH to the volume integral <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **ADMMass::ADMMass\_distance\_from\_grid\_boundary** | `REAL` | `-1.0` | distance between the grid boundaries and the surface of the integration box <br> *Tartomány:* `:` |
| **ADMMass::ADMMass\_number** | `INT` | `1` | number of measurements <br> *Tartomány:* `0:` |
| **ADMMass::ADMMass\_surface\_distance** | `REAL` | `-1.0` | distance between the above-defined center of the integration (cubic) box and its surface <br> *Tartomány:* `:` |
| **ADMMass::ADMMass\_use\_all\_volume\_as\_volume\_radius** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use the whole grid for volume integration <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **ADMMass::ADMMass\_use\_surface\_distance\_as\_volume\_radius** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Use ADMMass\_surface\_distance instead of ADMMass\_volume\_radius <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |

| **AHFinderDirect::run\_at\_CCTK\_ANALYSIS** | `BOOLEAN` | `false` | should we run at CCTK\_ANALYSIS? <br> *Tartomány:* `false` |
| **AHFinderDirect::run\_at\_CCTK\_POSTINITIAL** | `BOOLEAN` | `false` | should we run at CCTK\_POSTINITIAL? <br> *Tartomány:* `false` |
| **AHFinderDirect::run\_at\_CCTK\_POSTPOSTINITIAL** | `BOOLEAN` | `false` | should we run at CCTK\_POSTPOSTINITIAL? <br> *Tartomány:* `false` |
| **AHFinderDirect::run\_at\_CCTK\_POSTSTEP** | `BOOLEAN` | `true` | should we run at CCTK\_POSTSTEP? <br> *Tartomány:* `true` |
| **AHFinderDirect::run\_at\_CCTK\_POST\_RECOVER\_VARIABLES** | `BOOLEAN` | `false` | should we run at CCTK\_POST\_RECOVER\_VARIABLES? <br> *Tartomány:* `false` |
| **AHFinderDirect::set\_mask\_for\_all\_horizons** | `BOOLEAN` | `false` | should we set a mask grid function (or functions) for all horizons? <br> *Tartomány:* `false` |
| **AHFinderDirect::set\_mask\_for\_individual\_horizon** | `BOOLEAN` | `false` | should we set a mask grid function (or functions) for *this* horizon? <br> *Tartomány:* `should we set a mask grid function (or functions) for *this* horizon?` |
| **AHFinderDirect::set\_new\_style\_mask** | `BOOLEAN` | `false` | if we're setting a mask, should we set an new-style (CCTK\_INT) mask gridfn? <br> *Tartomány:* `false` |
| **AHFinderDirect::set\_old\_style\_mask** | `BOOLEAN` | `true` | if we're setting a mask, should we set an old-style (CCTK\_REAL) mask gridfn? <br> *Tartomány:* `true` |
| **AHFinderDirect::shiftout\_factor** | `REAL` | `1.0` | enlarge the surface initial guess before finding <br> *Tartomány:* `(0:*` |
| **AHFinderDirect::smoothing\_factor** | `REAL` | `0.0` | smoothen (remove higher multipole moments) the surface initial guess before finding <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **AHFinderDirect::surface\_definition** | `KEYWORD` | `expansion` | search for what kind of surface? <br> *Tartomány:* `Theta\_(l) Theta\_(n) (product of the expansions)` |
| **AHFinderDirect::surface\_interpolator\_name** | `STRING` | `Lagrange polynomial interpolation` | name under which the surface interpolation operator is registered in Cactus <br> *Tartomány:* `any string (in practice it should be nonempty)` |
| **AHFinderDirect::surface\_interpolator\_pars** | `STRING` | `order=2    boundary\_off\_centering\_tolerance={1.0e-10 1.0e-10 1.0e-10 1.0e-10}    boundary\_extrapolation\_tolerance={0.0 0.0 0.0 0.0}` | parameters for the surface interpolator <br> *Tartomány:* `.*` |
| **AHFinderDirect::surface\_modification** | `KEYWORD` | `none` | how to modify the surface definition <br> *Tartomány:* `multiply with the areal radius (does not converge nicely, because the Jacobian is only approximate)` |
| **AHFinderDirect::surface\_selection** | `KEYWORD` | `definition` | search for a surface with this areal radius <br> *Tartomány:* `look for a surface with a certain product of expansion and areal radius` |
| **AHFinderDirect::test\_all\_Jacobian\_compute\_methods** | `BOOLEAN` | `true` | should we test all Jacobian computation methods, or just NP? <br> *Tartomány:* `true` |
| **AHFinderDirect::track\_origin\_from\_grid\_scalar** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | track horizon origin from given grid scalars <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **AHFinderDirect::use\_pretracking** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | search for an outermost apparent horizon <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **AHFinderDirect::verbose\_level** | `KEYWORD` | `physics details` | controls which (how many) messages to print describing AH finding <br> *Tartomány:* `physics details + lots and lots of messages about the AH-finding algorithm` |
| **AHFinderDirect::want\_expansion\_gradients** | `BOOLEAN` | `false` | should we print the gradients of the expansions? <br> *Tartomány:* `false` |
| **AHFinderDirect::warn\_level\_\_gij\_not\_positive\_definite\_\_initial** | `INT` | `2` | warning level if the interpolated g\_{ij} isn't positive definite    (usually this means we're too close to a singularity)    (error occurs on first Newton iteration) <br> *Tartomány:* `-1:*` |
| **AHFinderDirect::warn\_level\_\_gij\_not\_positive\_definite\_\_subsequent** | `INT` | `2` | warning level if the interpolated g\_{ij} isn't positive definite    (usually this means we're too close to a singularity)    (error occurs on subsequent Newton iteration) <br> *Tartomány:* `-1:*` |
| **AHFinderDirect::warn\_level\_\_nonfinite\_geometry** | `INT` | `1` | warning level if we find infinity and/or NaN in the interpolated geometry    values {g\_ij, partial\_k g\_ij, K\_ij} <br> *Tartomány:* `-1:*` |
| **AHFinderDirect::warn\_level\_\_point\_outside\_\_initial** | `INT` | `1` | warning level for point outside (or too close to boundary of) Cactus grid    (error occurs on first Newton iteration) <br> *Tartomány:* `-1:*` |
| **AHFinderDirect::warn\_level\_\_point\_outside\_\_subsequent** | `INT` | `2` | warning level for point outside (or too close to boundary of) Cactus grid    (error occurs on subsequent Newton iteration) <br> *Tartomány:* `-1:*` |
| **AHFinderDirect::warn\_level\_\_skipping\_finite\_check** | `INT` | `3` | warning level if the user sets  check\_that\_geometry\_is\_finite    but the Cactus configure process doesn't find a  finite()  function    so we have to skip the finite-geometry check <br> *Tartomány:* `-1:*` |
| **AHFinderDirect::which\_horizon\_to\_announce\_centroid** | `INT` | `0` | for which horizon should we announce the centroid? <br> *Tartomány:* `1:100` |
| **AHFinderDirect::which\_surface\_to\_store\_info** | `INT` | `-1` | into which surface should we store the info? <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |

| **Cactus::max\_runtime** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Terminate evolution loop after a certain elapsed runtime (in minutes) <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:` |
| **Cactus::presync\_mode** | `KEYWORD` | `off` | behavior of the automatic pre-synchronization of grid fuctions <br> *Tartomány:* `The driver ignores SYNC statements and requires READS or WRITES for all variable access` |
| **Cactus::terminate** | `KEYWORD` | `iteration` | Condition on which to terminate evolution loop <br> *Tartomány:* `Take termination condition from both iteration number and coordinate time (DEPRECATED IN BETA14)` |
| **Cactus::terminate\_next** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Terminate on next iteration? <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **Cactus::allow\_mixeddim\_gfs** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Allow use of GFs from different dimensions <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **Cactus::cctk\_brief\_output** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Give only brief output <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **Cactus::cctk\_full\_warnings** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Give detailed information for each warning statement <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **Cactus::cctk\_show\_banners** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Show any registered banners for the different thorns <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **Cactus::cctk\_show\_schedule** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Print the scheduling tree to standard output <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **Cactus::cctk\_strong\_param\_check** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Die on parameter errors in CCTK\_PARAMCHECK <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **Cactus::cctk\_timer\_output** | `KEYWORD` | `off` | Provide output from timers <br> *Tartomány:* `Detailed timer output` |
| **Cactus::highlight\_warning\_messages** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Highlight CCTK warning messages ? <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **Cactus::info\_format** | `KEYWORD` | `basic` | What information do CCTK\_INFO() and CCTK\_VInfo() print? <br> *Tartomány:* `numeric\_timestamp\thuman readable timestamp: INFO (ThornName): message` |
| **Cactus::recovery\_mode** | `KEYWORD` | `strict` | How to behave when recovering from a checkpoint <br> *Tartomány:* `Recover variables if possible, after INITIAL timebins have been scheduled` |
| **Cactus::schedule\_sort\_mode** | `KEYWORD` | `none` | How to behave when no explicit ordering is prescribed between itemse <br> *Tartomány:* `sorted in reverse alphabetical order by thorn name and item name` |
| **Cactus::schedule\_sort\_warnings** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Output warnings if a schedule item refers to a non-existing item for ordering <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |

| **CanudaX\_BSSNMoL::conf\_fac\_floor** | `REAL` | `1.0d-04` | Minimal value of conformal factor <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **CanudaX\_BSSNMoL::derivs\_order** | `INT` | `4` | Accuracy of spatial finite difference stencils in RHS calculation <br> *Tartomány:* `8th order accurate stencils` |

| **CanudaX\_BSSNMoL::diss\_order** | `INT` | `5` | Accuracy of Kreiss-Oliger dissipation stencils; should be 1 highehr than derivs\_order <br> *Tartomány:* `N/A` |
| **CanudaX\_BSSNMoL::eta\_beta** | `REAL` | `1` | Damping parameter in live shift <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **CanudaX\_BSSNMoL::eta\_transition** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use an r-dependent damping parameter eta? <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |

| **CanudaX\_BSSNMoL::eta\_transition\_r** | `REAL` | `1` | Transition radius for r-dependent eta <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **CanudaX\_BSSNMoL::impose\_conf\_fac\_floor\_at\_initial** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use floor value on initial data? <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |

| **CanudaX\_BSSNMoL::zeta\_alpha** | `REAL` | `1` | Prefactor to the advection term in slicing condition <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **CanudaX\_BSSNMoL::zeta\_beta** | `REAL` | `1` | Prefactor to the advection term in shift condition <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |

| **CarpetLib::support\_staggered\_operators** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Provide one extra ghost point during restriction for staggered operators - EXPERIMENTAL <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **CarpetLib::use\_higher\_order\_restriction** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use third order cell centered restriction operators instead of first order <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **CarpetLib::barrier\_between\_stages** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Add a barrier between the communication stages (slows down, but may make timing numbers easier to interpret) <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **CarpetLib::barriers** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Insert barriers at strategic places for debugging purposes (slows down execution) <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **CarpetLib::check\_bboxes** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Check bounding box information for self-consistency <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **CarpetLib::check\_communication\_schedule** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Check the communication schedule at run time (expensive) <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **CarpetLib::combine\_recompose** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Recompose all grid functions of one refinement levels at once <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **CarpetLib::combine\_sends** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Send data together and in order of processor ranks <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |

| **CarpetLib::electric\_fence** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Surround each allocated memory block by canaries to check for out-of-bounds accesses <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **CarpetLib::fence\_width** | `INT` | `1` | number of guard cells to use <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |

| **CarpetLib::interpolate\_from\_buffer\_zones** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use buffer points for interpolation <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |

| **CarpetLib::max\_core\_size\_MB** | `INT` | `-2` | Maximum size of a core file, set via setrlimit <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |

| **CarpetLib::message\_count\_multiplier** | `INT` | `1` | Transmit messages this many times <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |
| **CarpetLib::message\_size\_multiplier** | `INT` | `1` | Enlarge size of transmitted messages by this factor <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |

| **CarpetLib::poison\_new\_memory** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Try to catch uninitialised data by setting newly allocated memory to values that will catch your attention <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **CarpetLib::print\_memstats\_every** | `INT` | `-1` | Report periodically how much memory is used per process <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |

| **CarpetLib::test\_backtrace** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Kill yourself to test the backtrace mechanism <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |

## 📦 Thorn: Coordinates<a id='coordinates'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **Coordinates::coordinate\_system** | `KEYWORD` | `Cartesian` | Available patch systems <br> *Tartomány:* `Cylindrical grid + central column` |
| **Coordinates::MonteCarloSeed** | `INT` | `1` | A seed for random number generator to get Monte Carlo particle distribution. <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::additional\_overlap\_size** | `INT` | `0` | Additional overlap between patches; this overlap is evolved, not interpolated <br> *Tartomány:* `N/A` |
| **Coordinates::additional\_symmetry\_size** | `INT` | `0` | Additional shiftout for symmetry boundaries <br> *Tartomány:* `0:1` |
| **Coordinates::box\_radius** | `REAL` | `3.0` | Half-size of Cartesian box <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::cubical\_inner\_boundary** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | give the inner boundary a cubical shape <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **Coordinates::cylinder\_inner\_radius** | `REAL` | `1.0` | Inner radius of the cylinder <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::cylinder\_outer\_radius** | `REAL` | `2.0` | Outer radius of the cylinder <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::cylinder\_radius** | `REAL` | `1.0` | Inner radius of cylinder <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::cylinder\_zmax** | `REAL` | `2.0` | Maximum z for cylinder and column <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **Coordinates::cylinder\_zmin** | `REAL` | `-2.0` | Minimum z for cylinder and column <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **Coordinates::h\_cartesian** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Inner cube resolution <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::h\_radial** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Radial resolution <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::h\_radial\_1** | `REAL` | `-1` | Intended radial resolution of the first stretched domain <br> *Tartomány:* `*` |
| **Coordinates::h\_radial\_inner** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Radial resolution for patches 1-6 of Thornburg13 <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::h\_radial\_outer** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Radial resolution for patches 7-13 of Thornburg13 <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::h\_z** | `REAL` | `0.1` | Spacing in z direction <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::internal\_outer\_boundaries** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Do the outer boundary points extend inwards? <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **Coordinates::nMonteCarloParticles** | `INT` | `500000` | Number of Monte-Carlo particles for determining fraction of cells that are on the nominal grid. This is used for the computation of the volume form. <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::n\_angular** | `INT` | `0` | Number of grid cells in the angular directions on the outer grids <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*:2` |
| **Coordinates::n\_angular\_inner** | `INT` | `0` | Number of gridpoints in angular directions on the patches 1-6 <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::n\_angular\_outer** | `INT` | `0` | Number of gridpoints in angular directions on the patches 7-13 <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::n\_angular\_phi** | `INT` | `40` | Number of angular points in the phi direction <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::n\_angular\_theta** | `INT` | `10` | Number of angular points in the theta direction <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::n\_xy** | `INT` | `10` | Number of points in xy-direction on the column patches <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::ncells\_x** | `INT` | `10` | Number of cells in the x direction <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::ncells\_y** | `INT` | `10` | Number of cells in the x direction <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::ncells\_z** | `INT` | `10` | Number of cells in the x direction <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::outer\_boundary\_size** | `INT` | `1` | Number of outer boundary points <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::patch\_boundary\_size** | `INT` | `1` | Number of inter-patch boundary points which are filled via interpolation (should be >= nghostzones) <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::patch\_one\_ncells\_x** | `INT` | `10` | Number of cells in the x direction for patch one <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::patch\_one\_ncells\_y** | `INT` | `10` | Number of cells in the y direction for patch one <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::patch\_one\_ncells\_z** | `INT` | `10` | Number of cells in the z direction for patch one <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::patch\_one\_xmax** | `REAL` | `1.0` | xmin for patch one <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **Coordinates::patch\_one\_xmin** | `REAL` | `0.0` | xmin for patch one <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **Coordinates::patch\_one\_ymax** | `REAL` | `0.5` | ymin for patch one <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **Coordinates::patch\_one\_ymin** | `REAL` | `-0.5` | ymin for patch one <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **Coordinates::patch\_one\_zmax** | `REAL` | `0.5` | zmin for patch one <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **Coordinates::patch\_one\_zmin** | `REAL` | `-0.5` | zmin for patch one <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **Coordinates::patch\_two\_ncells\_x** | `INT` | `10` | Number of cells in the x direction for patch two <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::patch\_two\_ncells\_y** | `INT` | `10` | Number of cells in the y direction for patch two <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::patch\_two\_ncells\_z** | `INT` | `10` | Number of cells in the z direction for patch two <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::patch\_two\_xmax** | `REAL` | `0.0` | xmin for patch two <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **Coordinates::patch\_two\_xmin** | `REAL` | `-1.0` | xmin for patch two <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **Coordinates::patch\_two\_ymax** | `REAL` | `0.5` | ymin for patch two <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **Coordinates::patch\_two\_ymin** | `REAL` | `-0.5` | ymin for patch two <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **Coordinates::patch\_two\_zmax** | `REAL` | `0.5` | zmin for patch two <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **Coordinates::patch\_two\_zmin** | `REAL` | `-0.5` | zmin for patch two <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **Coordinates::patch\_xmax** | `REAL` | `1.0` | xmin for the patch <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **Coordinates::patch\_xmin** | `REAL` | `0.0` | xmin for the patch <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **Coordinates::patch\_ymax** | `REAL` | `0.5` | ymin for the patch <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **Coordinates::patch\_ymin** | `REAL` | `-0.5` | ymin for the patch <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **Coordinates::patch\_zmax** | `REAL` | `0.5` | zmin for the patch <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **Coordinates::patch\_zmin** | `REAL` | `-0.5` | zmin for the patch <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **Coordinates::radial\_stretch** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Stretch the radial coordinate <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **Coordinates::register\_symmetry** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Register patch boundaries as symmetries <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **Coordinates::shiftout\_outer\_boundaries** | `INT` | `0` | Offset between the boundary location and the first outer boundary point <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **Coordinates::sphere\_inner\_radius** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Inner radius for the spherical grids <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::sphere\_medium\_radius** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Medium radius for the 13 patch system spherical grids <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::sphere\_outer\_radius** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Location of the physical outer boundary. <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::stagger\_outer\_boundaries** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Stagger the grid at the outer boundaries <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **Coordinates::stagger\_patch\_boundaries** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Stagger the grid at the inter-patch boundaries? <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **Coordinates::store\_inverse\_jacobian** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Numerically evaluate and store the transformation dx^i/da^k  (a: local, x: global) <br> *Tartomány:* `Numerically evaluate and store the transformation dx^i/da^k  (a: local, x: global)` |
| **Coordinates::store\_jacobian** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Numerically evaluate and store the transformation da^i/dx^k  (a: local, x: global) <br> *Tartomány:* `Numerically evaluate and store the transformation da^i/dx^k  (a: local, x: global)` |
| **Coordinates::store\_jacobian\_derivative** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Store the derivative of the Jacobian d^2[global]/d[local]^2 <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **Coordinates::store\_volume\_form** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Store determinant of Jacobian <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **Coordinates::stretch\_rmax\_1** | `REAL` | `2e10` | Outer radius of first stretching region <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::stretch\_rmin\_1** | `REAL` | `1e10` | Inner radius of first stretching region <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::symmetry** | `KEYWORD` | `full` | Select a symmetry <br> *Tartomány:* `octant mode (positive xyz)` |
| **Coordinates::theta\_min** | `REAL` | `10` | Minimal polar angle to cover by the spherical grid patch, in degrees <br> *Tartomány:* `(0:90)` |
| **Coordinates::transition\_radius** | `REAL` | `2.0` | Transition radius between box and cylinder <br> *Tartomány:* `(0:*` |
| **Coordinates::verbose** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Output information periodically <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |

## 📦 Thorn: CottonmouthBSSNOK4m<a id='cottonmouthbssnok4m'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **CottonmouthBSSNOK4m::apply\_NewRadX** | `BOOLEAN` | `false` | Apply NewRadX boundary conditions <br> *Tartomány:* `false` |
| **CottonmouthBSSNOK4m::conformal\_factor\_floor** | `REAL` | `1e-06` | The conformal factor W will never be smaller than this value <br> *Tartomány:* `*` |
| **CottonmouthBSSNOK4m::dissipation\_epsilon** | `REAL` | `0.32` | The ammount of dissipation to add. Should be in the [0, 1/3[ range <br> *Tartomány:* `*` |
| **CottonmouthBSSNOK4m::eta\_b** | `REAL` | `2.0` | Standard Gamma driver eta gauge parameter. Must be of order 2 / M\_ADM <br> *Tartomány:* `*` |
| **CottonmouthBSSNOK4m::evolved\_lapse\_floor** | `REAL` | `1e-08` | The evolved lapse will never be smaller than this value <br> *Tartomány:* `*` |
| **CottonmouthBSSNOK4m::radpower\_At** | `REAL` | `1.0` | NewRadX radpower for At\_ij <br> *Tartomány:* `*` |
| **CottonmouthBSSNOK4m::radpower\_ConfConnect** | `REAL` | `1.0` | NewRadX radpower Gamma^i <br> *Tartomány:* `*` |
| **CottonmouthBSSNOK4m::radpower\_chi** | `REAL` | `1.0` | NewRadX radpower for w <br> *Tartomány:* `*` |
| **CottonmouthBSSNOK4m::radpower\_evo\_lapse** | `REAL` | `1.0` | NewRadX radpower for the lapse, alpha <br> *Tartomány:* `*` |
| **CottonmouthBSSNOK4m::radpower\_evo\_shift** | `REAL` | `1.0` | NewRadX radpower for the shift, beta^i <br> *Tartomány:* `*` |
| **CottonmouthBSSNOK4m::radpower\_gt** | `REAL` | `1.0` | NewRadX radpower for gt\_ij <br> *Tartomány:* `*` |
| **CottonmouthBSSNOK4m::radpower\_shiftB** | `REAL` | `1.0` | NewRadX radpower for the aux. shift vector B^i <br> *Tartomány:* `*` |
| **CottonmouthBSSNOK4m::radpower\_trK** | `REAL` | `1.0` | NewRadX radpower for the trace of K\_ij <br> *Tartomány:* `*` |

| **Dissipation::epsdis\_for\_level** | `REAL` | `-1.0` | Alternate epsdis for a specific refinement level <br> *Tartomány:* `:` |

## 📦 Thorn: EHFinder<a id='ehfinder'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **EHFinder::EHFinder\_MaxNumEvolvedVars** | `INT` | `1` | The maximum number of evolved variables used by EHFinder <br> *Tartomány:* `1:10` |
| **EHFinder::EHFinder\_Max\_Evolved\_Array\_Size** | `INT` | `1` | The maximum size of evolved grid arrays used by EHFinder <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |
| **EHFinder::EHFinder\_Num\_ArrayEvolved\_Vars** | `INT` | `3` | The maximum number of evolved grid arrays used by EHFinder <br> *Tartomány:* `0:30` |
| **EHFinder::area\_calculation\_method** | `KEYWORD` | `standard` | How should the areas be calculated <br> *Tartomány:* `Using an isosurface triangulation` |
| **EHFinder::area\_interpolation\_order** | `INT` | `3` | What order should be used for the area interpoation <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |
| **EHFinder::area\_interpolator** | `STRING` | `Lagrange polynomial interpolation` | What interpolator should be used for the area <br> *Tartomány:* `A valid interpolator name` |
| **EHFinder::cas\_a** | `REAL` | `2.0` | Initial a coefficient of ovaloid of cassini <br> *Tartomány:* `:` |
| **EHFinder::cas\_b** | `REAL` | `2.05` | Initial b coefficient of ovaloid of cassini <br> *Tartomány:* `:` |
| **EHFinder::center\_x** | `REAL` | `0.0` | The x-coordinate of the center <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **EHFinder::center\_y** | `REAL` | `0.0` | The y-coordinate of the center <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **EHFinder::center\_z** | `REAL` | `0.0` | The z-coordinate of the center <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **EHFinder::cheat** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Should we cheat and evolve using the last data set for a while? <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **EHFinder::cheat\_iterations** | `INT` | `0` | For how many iterations should we cheat <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **EHFinder::eh\_lapse\_type** | `KEYWORD` | `numerical` | Do we use numerical or analytic metric information <br> *Tartomány:* `Use external analytic metric` |
| **EHFinder::eh\_metric\_type** | `KEYWORD` | `numerical` | Do we use numerical or analytic metric information <br> *Tartomány:* `Use external analytic metric` |
| **EHFinder::eh\_number\_level\_sets** | `INT` | `1` | How many level set functions should we evolve <br> *Tartomány:* `1:10` |
| **EHFinder::eh\_shift\_type** | `KEYWORD` | `numerical` | Do we use numerical or analytic metric information <br> *Tartomány:* `Use external analytic metric` |
| **EHFinder::evolve\_generators** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Should the generators be evolved <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **EHFinder::file\_type** | `KEYWORD` | `one\_file` | Are the timesteps in separate files or in one file? <br> *Tartomány:* `Timesteps are in separete files` |
| **EHFinder::generator\_distribution** | `KEYWORD` | `line` | What initial distribution should be used <br> *Tartomány:* `Put the generators on a surface with spherical topology` |
| **EHFinder::generator\_interpolation\_order** | `INT` | `3` | What order should be used for the generator interpoation <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |
| **EHFinder::generator\_interpolator** | `STRING` | `Lagrange polynomial interpolation` | What interpolator should be used for the generators <br> *Tartomány:* `A valid interpolator name` |
| **EHFinder::generator\_tracking\_method** | `KEYWORD` | `interpolate\_before` | What method should be used for tracking the generators <br> *Tartomány:* `Calculate first, then interpolate` |
| **EHFinder::initial\_a** | `REAL` | `1.0` | Initial a coefficient of ellipsoid <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:` |
| **EHFinder::initial\_b** | `REAL` | `1.0` | Initial b coefficient of ellipsoid <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:` |
| **EHFinder::initial\_c** | `REAL` | `1.0` | Initial c coefficient of ellipsoid <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:` |
| **EHFinder::initial\_f** | `KEYWORD` | `sphere` | Initial surface choice <br> *Tartomány:* `ovaloid of cassini` |
| **EHFinder::initial\_rad** | `REAL` | `1.0` | Initial radius of surface <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:` |
| **EHFinder::last\_iteration\_number** | `INT` | `0` | The starting iteration number for the EH\_Finder (last iteration number of the simulation) <br> *Tartomány:* `0:` |
| **EHFinder::maximum\_surface\_number** | `INT` | `1` | The maximum number of surfaces expected in the data <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |
| **EHFinder::mode** | `KEYWORD` | `normal` | Mode of operation <br> *Tartomány:* `Provide storage for f and initialize without evolving` |
| **EHFinder::n\_array\_ghosts** | `INT` | `1` | Number of ghost points in the surface grid array <br> *Tartomány:* `1:` |
| **EHFinder::nphi** | `INT` | `51` | Number of points in the phi direction when finding points on the surface <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*:2` |
| **EHFinder::ntheta** | `INT` | `51` | Number of points in the theta direction when finding points on the surface <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*:2` |
| **EHFinder::number\_of\_generators** | `INT` | `1` | How many generators should be evolved <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |
| **EHFinder::number\_of\_generators\_phi** | `INT` | `1` | How many generators in the phi direction <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |
| **EHFinder::number\_of\_generators\_theta** | `INT` | `1` | How many generators in the theta direction <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |
| **EHFinder::pde\_differences** | `KEYWORD` | `upwind2` | Type of finite diffencing used in pde re-initialization <br> *Tartomány:* `Use 2nd order upwinded differences everywhere` |
| **EHFinder::re\_init\_int\_method** | `KEYWORD` | `euler` | Integration method used in re-initialization <br> *Tartomány:* `Second order Runge-Kutta scheme` |
| **EHFinder::re\_init\_max\_iter** | `INT` | `800` | maximum number of iterations in the re-initialization <br> *Tartomány:* `0:` |
| **EHFinder::re\_init\_undo** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Should re-initialization be undone at pinch-off <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **EHFinder::re\_init\_verbose** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Should re-initialization be verbose? <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **EHFinder::re\_initialize\_every** | `INT` | `10` | How often to re-initialize the level set function <br> *Tartomány:* `0:` |
| **EHFinder::read\_conformal\_factor\_once** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Should the conformal factor only be read once <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **EHFinder::rotation\_alpha** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Rotation angle around z-axis of ellipsoid <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **EHFinder::rotation\_beta** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Rotation angle around y-axis of ellipsoid <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **EHFinder::rotation\_gamma** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Rotation angle around x-axis of ellipsoid <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **EHFinder::saved\_iteration\_every** | `INT` | `1` | How often was the numerical data saved <br> *Tartomány:* `1:` |
| **EHFinder::shell\_width** | `REAL` | `7.0` | Width of the evolution region in units of the grid spacing <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:` |
| **EHFinder::surface\_direction** | `KEYWORD` | `outward` | Should we track outward or inward moving surfaces <br> *Tartomány:* `Track inward moving surfaces.` |
| **EHFinder::surface\_interpolation\_order** | `INT` | `2` | What order should be used for the surface interpoation <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |
| **EHFinder::surface\_interpolator** | `STRING` | `Hermite polynomial interpolation` | What interpolator should be used to locate the surface <br> *Tartomány:* `A valid interpolator name` |
| **EHFinder::translate\_x** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Translation in x-direction <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **EHFinder::translate\_y** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Translation in y-direction <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **EHFinder::translate\_z** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Translation in z-direction <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **EHFinder::upwind\_type** | `KEYWORD` | `characteristic` | Type of upwinding used in evolving the ehfinder equations <br> *Tartomány:* `Use characteristic information` |
| **EHFinder::use\_inner\_excision** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Should inner excision be used? <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **EHFinder::use\_outer\_excision** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Should outer excision be used? <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **EHFinder::use\_user\_center** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Should the user prescribed center be used <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |

## 📦 Thorn: ET_BHaHAHA<a id='et_bhahaha'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::bah\_BBH\_mode\_common\_horizon\_idx** | `INT` | `2` | BBH mode: Horizon indices (zero-offset) for two inspiralling BHs. E.g., if the common horizon has index 2, then set bah\_BBH\_mode\_common\_horizon\_idx=2 <br> *Tartomány:* `0:2` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::bah\_BBH\_mode\_enable** | `INT` | `0` | 0 = disable; 1 = enable <br> *Tartomány:* `N/A` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::bah\_BBH\_mode\_inspiral\_BH\_idxs** | `INT` | `0` | BBH mode: Horizon indices (zero-offset) for two inspiralling BHs. E.g., if the inspiralling BHs have indices 0,1 then set BHaHAHA::bah\_BBH\_mode\_inspiral\_BH\_idxs = [0,1] <br> *Tartomány:* `0:2` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::bah\_KO\_strength** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Hyperbolic relaxation Kreiss-Oliger dissipation parameter. This usually hurts more than it helps, so is disabled (0.0) by default. <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::bah\_M\_scale** | `REAL` | `1.0` | Mass scale for each horizon. <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::bah\_Nphi\_array\_multigrid** | `INT` | `-100` | Multigrid Nphi array. <br> *Tartomány:* `2:*:2` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::bah\_Nr\_interp\_max** | `INT` | `48` | Maximum number of radial points for  grid(s): Number of points sampling radial direction. <br> *Tartomány:* `4:*` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::bah\_Ntheta\_array\_multigrid** | `INT` | `-100` | Multigrid Ntheta array. <br> *Tartomány:* `2:*:2` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::bah\_Theta\_L2\_times\_M\_tolerance** | `REAL` | `1e-2` | Primary convergence criterion: area-weighted L2 norm of residual function Theta on horizon. <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::bah\_Theta\_Linf\_times\_M\_tolerance** | `REAL` | `1e-5` | Secondary convergence criterion: L-infinity norm of Theta, time mass scale. <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::bah\_cfl\_factor** | `REAL` | `0.98` | Courant-Friedrichs-Lewy (CFL) factor to set timestep within BHaHAHA hyperbolic relaxation. 0.98 robust default, even for new horizon formation (e.g., common horizons). <br> *Tartomány:* `0:1.0` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::bah\_enable\_eta\_varying\_alg\_for\_precision\_common\_horizon** | `INT` | `0` | Enable eta-varying algorithm for high-precision common horizon finding. For general-purpose horizon finding, leave this disabled. <br> *Tartomány:* `0:1` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::bah\_eta\_damping\_times\_M** | `REAL` | `7.0` | Hyperbolic relaxation exponential damping parameter. <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::bah\_initial\_grid\_x\_center** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Initial x locations of horizon grid centers <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::bah\_initial\_grid\_y\_center** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Initial y locations of horizon grid centers <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::bah\_initial\_grid\_z\_center** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Initial z locations of horizon grid centers <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::bah\_max\_iterations** | `INT` | `10000` | Maximum hyperbolic relaxation steps before ending the horizon search. <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::bah\_max\_search\_radius** | `REAL` | `1.5` | Input spherical grid(s): maximum radius to search, for all time. <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::bah\_num\_resolutions\_multigrid** | `INT` | `3` | Number of resolutions provided in bah\_N{theta,phi}\_array\_multigrid[] arrays <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::bah\_verbosity\_level** | `INT` | `1` | 0 = essential only; 1 = physical summary of found horizon; 2 = full algorithmic details <br> *Tartomány:* `N/A` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::find\_every** | `INT` | `1` | Search cadence for all horizons. <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::find\_every\_individual** | `INT` | `-1` | Search cadence for individual apparent horizons. (overrides find\_every if >= 1). <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::interpolator\_name** | `STRING` | `Hermite polynomial interpolation` | Which interpolator should I use <br> *Tartomány:* `Any nonempty string` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::interpolator\_pars** | `STRING` | `order=3` | Parameters for the interpolator <br> *Tartomány:* `.*` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::max\_Nphi** | `INT` | `64` | Maximum number of points that will sample the phi direction. For typical horizon finding, leave this to default value of 64. <br> *Tartomány:* `2:256:2` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::max\_Ntheta** | `INT` | `32` | Maximum number of points that will sample the theta direction. For typical horizon finding, leave this to default value of 32. <br> *Tartomány:* `2:128:2` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::max\_num\_horizons** | `INT` | `3` | Maximum number of horizons for this simulation. <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::sync\_data\_across\_ranks\_after\_each\_search\_\_warning\_slow** | `INT` | `0` | Enables restart from a different number of MPI ranks. WARNING: Slows BHaHAHA by >2x <br> *Tartomány:* `0:1` |
| **ET\_BHaHAHA::which\_surface\_to\_store\_info** | `INT` | `-1` | Which SphericalSurface index should receive this horizon (-1 disables export). BHaHAHA horizon numbering is zero-based. On multi-rank runs, enabling SphericalSurface export forces a post-find sync and may carry the same cost as sync\_data\_across\_ranks\_after\_each\_search\_\_warning\_slow. <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |

## 📦 Thorn: FLRWSolver<a id='flrwsolver'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **FLRWSolver::FLRW\_boxlength** | `REAL` | `1.0` | Size of box used in comoving Mpc -- only used in generating power spectrum perturbations <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:*` |
| **FLRWSolver::FLRW\_init\_HL** | `REAL` | `1.0` | FLRW initial H\_* L -- ratio of box length to horizon size. This sets initial background density <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:*` |
| **FLRWSolver::FLRW\_init\_a** | `REAL` | `1.0` | FLRW initial scale factor <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0001:*` |
| **FLRWSolver::FLRW\_lapse\_value** | `REAL` | `1.0` | Value of background initial lapse (alpha) -- for perturbations this becomes alp=FLRW\_lapse\_value*sqrt(1 + 2 Phi) <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **FLRWSolver::FLRW\_perturb** | `KEYWORD` | `no` | Add perturbation to matter and metric <br> *Tartomány:* `Do not add perturbation, will run FLRW spacetime` |
| **FLRWSolver::FLRW\_perturb\_direction** | `KEYWORD` | `all` | Choose which direction to perturb (only used if FLRW\_perturb\_type = single\_mode) <br> *Tartomány:* `perturb all dimensions` |
| **FLRWSolver::FLRW\_perturb\_type** | `KEYWORD` | `single\_mode` | Add perturbation of the following type <br> *Tartomány:* `powerspectrum\_exact` |
| **FLRWSolver::FLRW\_random\_seed** | `INT` | `10` | Random seed for generating the Gaussian random density perturbation from the power spectrum given in FLRW\_powerspectrum\_file. <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **FLRWSolver::phi\_amplitude** | `REAL` | `1.e-8` | Amplitude of perturbation in phi - implicitly sets amplitude of rho,vel perturbation <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:*` |
| **FLRWSolver::phi\_phase\_offset** | `REAL` | `0.0` | The phase offset, eta, in phi=A*sin(kx*x - eta) for single mode perturbation -- applied same in all dimentions if FLRW\_perturb\_direction = all <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **FLRWSolver::single\_perturb\_wavelength** | `REAL` | `1.0` | Wavelength of single mode scalar perturbation relative to box size <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0001:*` |

## 📦 Thorn: FishboneMoncriefID<a id='fishbonemoncriefid'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **FishboneMoncriefID::A\_b** | `REAL` | `1.0` | Scaling factor for the vector potential <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **FishboneMoncriefID::M** | `REAL` | `1.0` | Kerr-Schild BH mass. Probably should always set M=1. <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:*` |
| **FishboneMoncriefID::a** | `REAL` | `0.9375` | The spin parameter of the black hole <br> *Tartomány:* `0:1.0` |
| **FishboneMoncriefID::gamma** | `REAL` | `1.3333333333333333333333333333` | Equation of state: P = kappa * rho^gamma <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:*` |
| **FishboneMoncriefID::kappa** | `REAL` | `1.0e-3` | Equation of state: P = kappa * rho^gamma <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:*` |
| **FishboneMoncriefID::r\_at\_max\_density** | `REAL` | `12.0` | Radius at maximum disk density. Needs to be > r\_in <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:*` |
| **FishboneMoncriefID::r\_in** | `REAL` | `6.0` | Fixes the inner edge of the disk <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:*` |
| **FishboneMoncriefID::random\_max** | `REAL` | `0.02` | Ceiling value of random perturbation to initial pressure, where perturbed pressure = pressure*(1.0 + (random\_min + (random\_max-random\_min)*RAND[0,1))) <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **FishboneMoncriefID::random\_min** | `REAL` | `-0.02` | Floor value of random perturbation to initial pressure, where perturbed pressure = pressure*(1.0 + (random\_min + (random\_max-random\_min)*RAND[0,1))) <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |

| **GRHayLID::stagger\_A\_fields** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Sets whether Avec is staggered to +1/2 (following IllinoisGRMHD convention) <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **GRHayLID::wave\_amplitude** | `REAL` | `1.0e-3` | Wave amplitude for sound wave initial data <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:*` |

| **GRHayLib::P\_atm** | `REAL` | `-1` | Atmospheric pressure (required) <br> *Tartomány:* `Used to check if anything was given` |

| **GRHayLib::Psi6threshold** | `REAL` | `1e100` | Where Psi^6 > Psi6threshold, we assume we're inside the horizon in the primitives solver, and certain limits are relaxed or imposed <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **GRHayLib::T\_atm** | `REAL` | `-1` | Atmospheric temperature (required) <br> *Tartomány:* `Used to check if anything was given` |

| **GRHayLib::T\_min** | `REAL` | `-1` | Minimum temperature allowed (optional; table bounds used by default) <br> *Tartomány:* `N/A` |
| **GRHayLib::Y\_e\_atm** | `REAL` | `-1` | Atmospheric electron fraction (required) <br> *Tartomány:* `Used to check if anything was given` |
| **GRHayLib::Y\_e\_max** | `REAL` | `-1` | Atmospheric electron fraction (optional; table bounds used by default) <br> *Tartomány:* `N/A` |
| **GRHayLib::Y\_e\_min** | `REAL` | `-1` | Atmospheric electron fraction (optional; table bounds used by default) <br> *Tartomány:* `N/A` |
| **GRHayLib::calc\_primitive\_guess** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Do we want to calculate estimates for the primitives in Con2Prim? <br> *Tartomány:* `Calculates primitive guesses from the current conservatives.` |
| **GRHayLib::con2prim\_backup\_routines** | `KEYWORD` | `None` | We allow up to 3 backup routines <br> *Tartomány:* `https://escholarship.org/content/qt0s53f84b/qt0s53f84b.pdf (see also https://arxiv.org/pdf/1712.07538.pdf)` |

| **GRHayLib::k\_ppoly0** | `REAL` | `-1` | Also known as k\_ppoly[0], this is the polytropic constant for the lowest density piece of the piecewise polytrope. All other k\_ppoly elements are set using rho\_ppoly\_in and enforcing continuity in the equation of state. <br> *Tartomány:* `forbidden value to make sure it is explicitly set in the parfile` |

| **GRHayLib::neos** | `INT` | `1` | number of parameters in EOS table <br> *Tartomány:* `1:10` |
| **GRHayLib::ppm\_flattening\_epsilon** | `REAL` | `0.33` | Epsilon used for flattening in PPM algorithm <br> *Tartomány:* `:` |
| **GRHayLib::ppm\_flattening\_omega1** | `REAL` | `0.75` | Omega1 used for flattening in PPM algorithm <br> *Tartomány:* `:` |
| **GRHayLib::ppm\_flattening\_omega2** | `REAL` | `10.0` | Omega2 used for flattening in PPM algorithm <br> *Tartomány:* `:` |
| **GRHayLib::ppm\_shock\_epsilon** | `REAL` | `0.01` | Epsilon used for shock detection in steepen\_rho algorithm <br> *Tartomány:* `:` |
| **GRHayLib::ppm\_shock\_eta1** | `REAL` | `20.0` | Eta1 used for shock detection in steepen\_rho algorithm <br> *Tartomány:* `:` |
| **GRHayLib::ppm\_shock\_eta2** | `REAL` | `0.05` | Eta2 used for shock detection in steepen\_rho algorithm <br> *Tartomány:* `:` |
| **GRHayLib::ppm\_shock\_k0** | `REAL` | `0.1` | K0 used for shock detection in steepen\_rho algorithm <br> *Tartomány:* `:` |

| **GRHayLib::rho\_b\_max** | `REAL` | `-1` | Ceiling value on the baryonic rest mass density rho\_b (disabled by default). Can help with stability to limit densities inside the BH after black hole formation. <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **GRHayLib::rho\_b\_min** | `REAL` | `-1` | Floor value on the baryonic rest mass density rho\_b (disabled by default). <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **GRHayLib::rho\_ppoly\_in** | `REAL` | `-1` | Set polytropic rho parameters <br> *Tartomány:* `forbidden value to make sure it is explicitly set in the parfile (only checked for neos > 1)` |

## 📦 Thorn: GRHydro<a id='grhydro'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **GRHydro::Avec\_gauge** | `KEYWORD` | `lorenz` | Which gauge condition to use when evolving the vector potential <br> *Tartomány:* `Lorenz gauge` |
| **GRHydro::Check\_Rho\_Minimum** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Should a check on rho < GRHydro\_rho\_min be performed and written as WARNING level 2? <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::EoS\_Change** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Recalculate fluid quantities if changing the EoS <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::EoS\_Change\_type** | `KEYWORD` | `Gamma` | Change polytropic K or Gamma? <br> *Tartomány:* `Change K and Gamma, Shibata et al. 2004 3-D GR Core Collapse style` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro\_MaxNumConstrainedVars** | `INT` | `37` | The maximum number of constrained variables used by GRHydro <br> *Tartomány:* `7:48` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro\_MaxNumEvolvedVars** | `INT` | `5` | The maximum number of evolved variables used by GRHydro <br> *Tartomány:* `5:12` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro\_MaxNumEvolvedVarsSlow** | `INT` | `0` | The maximum number of evolved variables used by GRHydro <br> *Tartomány:* `5:12` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro\_MaxNumSandRVars** | `INT` | `16` | The maximum number of save and restore variables used by GRHydro <br> *Tartomány:* `0:16` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro\_NaN\_verbose** | `INT` | `2` | The warning level for NaNs occuring within GRHydro <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro\_Y\_e\_max** | `REAL` | `1.0` | maximum allowed Y\_e <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:*` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro\_Y\_e\_min** | `REAL` | `0.0` | minimum allowed Y\_e <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:*` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro\_atmo\_tolerance** | `REAL` | `0.0` | A point is set to atmosphere in the Con2Prim's if its rho < GRHydro\_rho\_min *(1+GRHydro\_atmo\_tolerance). This avoids occasional spurious oscillations in carpet buffer zones lying in the atmosphere (because prolongation happens on conserved variables) <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro\_c2p\_failed\_action** | `KEYWORD` | `abort` | what to do when we detect a c2p failure <br> *Tartomány:* `request termination` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro\_c2p\_reset\_eps\_tau\_hot\_eos** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | As a last resort, reset tau <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro\_c2p\_warn\_from\_reflevel** | `INT` | `0` | Start warning on given refinement level and on higher levels <br> *Tartomány:* `0:` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro\_c2p\_warnlevel** | `INT` | `0` | Warnlevel for Con2Prim warnings <br> *Tartomány:* `0:1` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro\_countmax** | `INT` | `100` | Maximum number of iterations for Con2Prim solve <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro\_countmin** | `INT` | `1` | Minimum number of iterations for Con2Prim solve <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro\_del\_ptol** | `REAL` | `1.e-18` | Tolerance for primitive variable solve (absolute) <br> *Tartomány:* `0:` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro\_enable\_internal\_excision** | `BOOLEAN` | `true` | Set this to 'false' to disable the thorn-internal excision. <br> *Tartomány:* `true` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro\_eos\_hot\_eps\_fix** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Activate quasi-failsafe mode for hot EOSs <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro\_eos\_hot\_prim2con\_warn** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Warn about temperature workaround in prim2con <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |

| **GRHydro::GRHydro\_hot\_atmo\_Y\_e** | `REAL` | `0.5e0` | Y\_e of the hot atmosphere <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:*` |

| **GRHydro::GRHydro\_lorentz\_overshoot\_cutoff** | `REAL` | `1.e100` | Set the Lorentz factor to this value in case it overshoots (1/0) <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro\_max\_temp** | `REAL` | `90.0e0` | maximum temperature we allow <br> *Tartomány:* `(0.0:*` |

| **GRHydro::GRHydro\_perc\_ptol** | `REAL` | `1.e-8` | Tolerance for primitive variable solve (percent) <br> *Tartomány:* `0:` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro\_polish** | `INT` | `0` | Number of extra iterations after root found <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |

| **GRHydro::HLLE\_type** | `KEYWORD` | `Standard` | Which HLLE type to use <br> *Tartomány:* `Tadmor's simplification of HLLE` |
| **GRHydro::Tmunu\_damping\_radius\_max** | `REAL` | `-1` | damping radius at which Tmunu becomes 0 <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::Tmunu\_damping\_radius\_min** | `REAL` | `-1` | damping radius at which we start to damp with a tanh function <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::WENO\_order** | `INT` | `5` | The order of accuracy of the WENO reconstruction <br> *Tartomány:* `Fifth-order` |
| **GRHydro::apply\_H\_viscosity** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | H viscosity is useful to fix the carbuncle instability seen at strong shocks <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::atmo\_falloff\_power** | `REAL` | `0.0` | The power at which the atmosphere level falls off as (atmo\_falloof\_radius/r)**atmo\_falloff\_power <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::atmo\_falloff\_radius** | `REAL` | `50.0` | The radius for which the atmosphere starts to falloff as (atmo\_falloff\_radius/r)**atmo\_falloff\_power <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::atmo\_tolerance\_power** | `REAL` | `0.0` | The power at which the atmosphere tolerance increases as (r/atmo\_tolerance\_radius)**atmo\_tolerance\_power <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::atmo\_tolerance\_radius** | `REAL` | `50.0` | The radius for which the atmosphere tolerance starts to increase as (r/atmo\_tolerance\_radius)**atmo\_tolerance\_power <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::bound** | `KEYWORD` | `none` | Which boundary condition to use - FIXME <br> *Tartomány:* `Constant` |
| **GRHydro::c2p\_reset\_pressure** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | If the pressure guess is unphysical should we recompute it? <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::c2p\_reset\_pressure\_to\_value** | `REAL` | `1.e-20` | The value to which the pressure is reset to when a failure occurrs in C2P <br> *Tartomány:* `0:` |
| **GRHydro::c2p\_resort\_to\_bisection** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | If the pressure guess is unphysical, should we try with bisection (slower, but more robust) <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::calculate\_bcom** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Calculate the comoving contravariant magnetic 4-vector b^a? <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::check\_for\_trivial\_rp** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Do check for trivial Riemann Problem <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **GRHydro::clean\_divergence** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use hyperbolic divergence cleaning <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::comoving\_attenuate** | `KEYWORD` | `tanh` | Which attenuation method for the comoving shift <br> *Tartomány:* `Multiply by 1/2*(1+tanh(factor*(rho/rho\_max - offset)))` |
| **GRHydro::comoving\_factor** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Factor multiplying the velocity for the comoving shift <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:2.0` |
| **GRHydro::comoving\_tanh\_factor** | `REAL` | `10.0` | The factor in the above tanh attenuation <br> *Tartomány:* `(0.0:*` |

| **GRHydro::comoving\_v\_method** | `KEYWORD` | `projected` | Which method for getting the radial velocity <br> *Tartomány:* `vr = sqrt(v\_i v^i)` |
| **GRHydro::con2prim\_oct\_hack** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Disregard c2p failures in oct/rotsym90 boundary cells with xyz < 0 <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::decouple\_normal\_Bfield** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | when using divergence cleaning properly decouple Bx,psidc subsystem <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **GRHydro::enhanced\_ppm\_C2** | `REAL` | `1.25` | Parameter for enhancecd ppm limiter. Default from McCorquodale & Colella 2011 <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |

| **GRHydro::evolve\_tracer** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Should we advect tracers? <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::gradient\_method** | `KEYWORD` | `First diff` | Which method is used to set GRHydro::DiffRho? <br> *Tartomány:* `Based on the size of rho` |
| **GRHydro::initial\_Gamma** | `REAL` | `1.3333` | If changing Gamma, what was the value used in the initial data routine? <br> *Tartomány:* `(0.0:` |
| **GRHydro::initial\_atmosphere\_factor** | `REAL` | `-1.0` | A relative (to the initial atmosphere) value for rho in the atmosphere. This is used at initial time only. Unused if negative. <br> *Tartomány:* `-1.0:` |
| **GRHydro::initial\_k** | `REAL` | `100.0` | If changing K, what was the value used in the initial data routine? <br> *Tartomány:* `(0.0:` |
| **GRHydro::initial\_rho\_abs\_min** | `REAL` | `-1.0` | An absolute value for rho in the atmosphere. To be used by initial data routines only. Unused if negative. <br> *Tartomány:* `-1.0:` |
| **GRHydro::initial\_rho\_rel\_min** | `REAL` | `-1.0` | A relative (to the central density) value for rho in the atmosphere. To be used by initial data routines only. Unused if negative. <br> *Tartomány:* `-1.0:` |
| **GRHydro::kap\_dc** | `REAL` | `10.0` | The kap parameter for divergence cleaning <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::left\_eigenvectors** | `KEYWORD` | `analytical` | How to compute the left eigenvectors <br> *Tartomány:* `Numerical left eigenvectors` |
| **GRHydro::max\_magnetic\_to\_gas\_pressure\_ratio** | `REAL` | `-1.0` | consider pressure to be magnetically dominated if magnetic pressure to gas pressure ratio is higher than this <br> *Tartomány:* `disable` |
| **GRHydro::method\_type** | `KEYWORD` | `RSA FV` | Which type of method to use <br> *Tartomány:* `Finite difference using Lax-Friedrichs flux splitting` |

Original algorithm does not use this. <br> *Tartomány:* `Same as WENO adaptive epsilon: adaptively reduce mp5\_eps according to norm of stencil. Original algorithm does not use this.` |

| **GRHydro::number\_of\_particles** | `INT` | `0` | Number of particles to track <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::number\_of\_tracers** | `INT` | `0` | Number of tracer variables to be advected <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |

| **GRHydro::ppm\_epsilon** | `REAL` | `0.33` | Epsilon for PPM zone flattening <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:` |
| **GRHydro::ppm\_epsilon\_shock** | `REAL` | `0.01` | Epsilon for PPM shock detection <br> *Tartomány:* `:` |

| **GRHydro::rho\_rel\_min** | `REAL` | `1.e-9` | A minimum relative rho (rhomin = centden * rho\_rel\_min) below which evolution is turned off (atmosphere). Only used if rho\_abs\_min < 0.0 <br> *Tartomány:* `0:` |

| **GRHydro::setcharmin** | `REAL` | `-1.0` | Minimum characteristic speed for psidc if psidcspeed is set <br> *Tartomány:* `-1:0` |
| **GRHydro::sources\_spatial\_order** | `INT` | `2` | Order of spatial differencing of the source terms <br> *Tartomány:* `4th order finite differencing` |
| **GRHydro::sqrtdet\_thr** | `REAL` | `-1.0` | Threshold to apply cons rescalings deep inside the horizon <br> *Tartomány:* `Do not apply limit` |
| **GRHydro::sync\_conserved\_only** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Only sync evolved conserved quantities during evolution. <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::tau\_rel\_min** | `REAL` | `1.e-10` | A minimum relative tau (taumin = maxtau(t=0) * tau\_rel\_min) below which tau is reschaled <br> *Tartomány:* `0:` |
| **GRHydro::track\_divB** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Track the magnetic field constraint violations <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::transport\_constraints** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use constraint transport for magnetic field <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::tvd\_limiter** | `KEYWORD` | `minmod` | Which slope limiter to use <br> *Tartomány:* `Superbee` |
| **GRHydro::use\_MoL\_slow\_multirate\_sector** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Whether to make use of MoL's slow multirate sector <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::use\_enhanced\_ppm** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use the enhanced ppm reconstruction method proposed by Colella & Sekora 2008 and McCorquodale & Colella 2011 <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::use\_evolution\_mask** | `KEYWORD` | `never` | Set this to 'always' to skip validity tests in regions where CarpetEvolutionMask::evolution\_mask vanishes. <br> *Tartomány:* `do not use the mask` |
| **GRHydro::use\_min\_tracer** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Should there be a floor on the tracer? <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::use\_optimized\_ppm** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | use C++ templated version of PPM. Experimental <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::use\_weighted\_fluxes** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Weight the flux terms by the cell surface areas <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::weno\_adaptive\_epsilon** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | use modified smoothness indicators that take into account scale of solution (adaptive epsilon) <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **GRHydro::weno\_eps** | `REAL` | `1e-26` | WENO epsilon parameter. For WENO-z, 1e-40 is recommended <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::wk\_atmosphere** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use some of Wolfgang Kastauns atmosphere tricks <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::constrain\_to\_1D** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Set fluid velocities to zero for non-radial motion <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::use\_cxx\_code** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Use experimental C++ code? <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **GRHydro::verbose** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Some debug output <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |

## 📦 Thorn: GiRaFFE<a id='giraffe'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **GiRaFFE::EM\_BC** | `KEYWORD` | `copy` | EM field boundary condition <br> *Tartomány:* `Frozen boundaries` |
| **GiRaFFE::GAMMA\_SPEED\_LIMIT** | `REAL` | `2000.0` | Maximum relativistic gamma factor. Note the default is much higher than IllinoisGRMHD. (GRFFE can handle higher Lorentz factors) <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |
| **GiRaFFE::Sym\_Bz** | `REAL` | `1.0` | In-progress equatorial symmetry support: Symmetry parameter across z axis for magnetic fields = +/- 1 <br> *Tartomány:* `-1.0:1.0` |
| **GiRaFFE::Symmetry** | `KEYWORD` | `none` | Currently only no symmetry supported, though work has begun in adding equatorial-symmetry support. FIXME: Extend ET symmetry interface to support symmetries on staggered gridfunctions <br> *Tartomány:* `no symmetry, full 3d domain` |
| **GiRaFFE::Velocity\_BC** | `KEYWORD` | `outflow` | Chosen fluid velocity boundary condition <br> *Tartomány:* `Frozen boundaries` |
| **GiRaFFE::current\_sheet\_null\_v** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Shall we null the velocity normal to the current sheet? <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **GiRaFFE::min\_radius\_inside\_of\_which\_conserv\_to\_prims\_FFE\_and\_FFE\_evolution\_is\_DISABLED** | `REAL` | `-1.` | As parameter suggests, this is the minimum radius inside of which the conservatives-to-primitives solver is disabled. In the Aligned Rotator test, this should be set equal to R\_NS\_aligned\_rotator. <br> *Tartomány:* `(0:*` |
| **GiRaFFE::damp\_lorenz** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Damping factor for the generalized Lorenz gauge. Has units of 1/length = 1/M. Typically set this parameter to 1.5/(maximum Delta t on AMR grids). <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |

| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **GiRaFFEfood::B\_p\_aligned\_rotator** | `REAL` | `1e-5` | The magnitude of the poloidal magnetic field in the aligned rotator test. <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |
| **GiRaFFEfood::Compute\_Exact\_Every** | `INT` | `1` | How often do we compute difference between exact and numerical (delAi quantities)? <br> *Tartomány:* `-1:*` |

## 📦 Thorn: Hydro_Analysis<a id='hydro_analysis'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **Hydro\_Analysis::restmass\_compute\_masses** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Should we compute the masses? <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **Hydro\_Analysis::restmass\_masses\_nr** | `INT` | `0` | number of radii within which to compute the rest mass <br> *Tartomány:* `0:100` |
| **Hydro\_Analysis::restmass\_ref\_radius\_mass** | `REAL` | `10.0` | Radii within which mass will be computed <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:` |
| **Hydro\_Analysis::restmass\_rho\_min** | `REAL` | `1.e-9` | Points with rest-mass density below this value are excluded (it could be set equal to the atmosphere value) <br> *Tartomány:* `0:` |
| **Hydro\_Analysis::Hydro\_Analysis\_average\_multiple\_maxima\_locations** | `BOOLEAN` | `false` | when finding more than one global maximum location, average position and use result <br> *Tartomány:* `false` |
| **Hydro\_Analysis::Hydro\_Analysis\_comp\_core\_rho\_centroid** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | compute location of the centroid of rho*x in region r\_core around densest point <br> *Tartomány:* `compute location of the centroid of rho*x in region r\_core around densest point` |
| **Hydro\_Analysis::Hydro\_Analysis\_comp\_rho\_max** | `BOOLEAN` | `false` | Look for the value and location of the maximum of rho <br> *Tartomány:* `false` |
| **Hydro\_Analysis::Hydro\_Analysis\_comp\_rho\_max\_every** | `INT` | `1` | How often to look  for the value and location of the maximum of rho <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |
| **Hydro\_Analysis::Hydro\_Analysis\_comp\_rho\_max\_origin\_distance** | `BOOLEAN` | `false` | Look for the proper distance between the maximum of the density and the origin (along a straight coordinate line) <br> *Tartomány:* `false` |
| **Hydro\_Analysis::Hydro\_Analysis\_comp\_vol\_weighted\_center\_of\_mass** | `BOOLEAN` | `false` | Look for the location of the volume-weighted center of mass <br> *Tartomány:* `false` |
| **Hydro\_Analysis::Hydro\_Analysis\_core\_rho\_rel\_min** | `REAL` | `0.01` | only include points where rho>rho\_rel\_min*rho\_max when computing centroid of rho*x around densest point <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Hydro\_Analysis::Hydro\_Analysis\_interpolator\_coordinates** | `STRING` | `cart3d` | Coordinate system <br> *Tartomány:* `.*` |
| **Hydro\_Analysis::Hydro\_Analysis\_interpolator\_name** | `STRING` | `uniform cartesian` | Name of the interpolator <br> *Tartomány:* `.*` |
| **Hydro\_Analysis::Hydro\_Analysis\_interpolator\_options** | `STRING` | `order=2` | Options for the interpolator <br> *Tartomány:* `.*` |
| **Hydro\_Analysis::Hydro\_Analysis\_r\_core** | `REAL` | `4.0` | size of region around densest point in which to compute the centroid of rho*x <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **Hydro\_Analysis::Hydro\_Analysis\_rho\_max\_loc\_only\_positive\_x** | `BOOLEAN` | `false` | Restrict location search for density maximum to positive values of x <br> *Tartomány:* `false` |
| **Hydro\_Analysis::Hydro\_Analysis\_rho\_max\_loc\_use\_rotatingsymmetry180** | `BOOLEAN` | `false` | Map found maxima into positive x half-plane assuming pi-symmetry <br> *Tartomány:* `false` |
| **Hydro\_Analysis::Hydro\_Analysis\_rho\_max\_origin\_distance\_npoints** | `INT` | `100` | Number of points along the straight line for measuring proper distance <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |
| **Hydro\_Analysis::verbosity\_level** | `INT` | `1` | how much information to ouptut to the logs <br> *Tartomány:* `also output location of maxima` |

| **LeanBSSNMoL::beta\_f\_delta2** | `REAL` | `0.0005` | delta2 coefficient for the shift condition of 1702.01755 <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |

| **LeanBSSNMoL::conf\_fac\_floor** | `REAL` | `1.0d-04` | Minimal value of conformal factor <br> *Tartomány:* `*:*` |

## 📦 Thorn: LoopControl<a id='loopcontrol'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **LoopControl::align\_with\_cachelines** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Align innermost loops with cache line size <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **LoopControl::explore\_eagerly\_before\_iteration** | `INT` | `0` | Try to explore the parameter space as much as possible before this iteration <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **LoopControl::initial\_setup** | `KEYWORD` | `tiled` | Initial configuration <br> *Tartomány:* `Basic LoopControl setup` |
| **LoopControl::loopsize\_i** | `INT` | `8` | Size of each thread's loop in i direction (in grid points) for multithreading <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |
| **LoopControl::loopsize\_j** | `INT` | `8` | Size of each thread's loop in j direction (in grid points) for multithreading <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |
| **LoopControl::loopsize\_k** | `INT` | `8` | Size of each thread's loop in k direction (in grid points) for multithreading <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |
| **LoopControl::max\_size\_factor** | `INT` | `4` | Maximum size for modifying loop sizes <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |
| **LoopControl::random\_jump\_probability** | `REAL` | `0.1` | Probability of a random jump to begin exploring a very different param <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:1.0` |
| **LoopControl::selftest** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Run a self test with every loop (expensive) <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **LoopControl::settle\_after\_iteration** | `INT` | `0` | Do not explore the parameter space any more at or after this iteration <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **LoopControl::statistics\_every\_seconds** | `REAL` | `-1.0` | Output statistics every so many seconds <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:*` |
| **LoopControl::statistics\_filename** | `STRING` | `LoopControl-statistics` | File name for LoopControl statistics <br> *Tartomány:* `,` |
| **LoopControl::tilesize\_i** | `INT` | `4` | Tile size in i direction (in grid points) for loop tiling <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |
| **LoopControl::tilesize\_j** | `INT` | `4` | Tile size in j direction (in grid points) for loop tiling <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |
| **LoopControl::tilesize\_k** | `INT` | `4` | Tile size in k direction (in grid points) for loop tiling <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |
| **LoopControl::tryout\_iterations** | `INT` | `1` | Try out new params for this many iterations before judging them <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |
| **LoopControl::use\_smt\_threads** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Place SMT threads close together <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **LoopControl::verbose** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Output some loop information at run time <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **LoopControl::very\_expensive\_factor** | `REAL` | `1.5` | Params worse than the current-best by more than this factor are ignored more quickly <br> *Tartomány:* `1.0:*` |
| **LoopControl::veryverbose** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Output detailed debug information at run time <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |

## 📦 Thorn: MoL<a id='mol'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **MoL::MoL\_Max\_Evolved\_Array\_Size** | `INT` | `0` | The maximum total size of any grid arrays to be evolved <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **MoL::MoL\_Num\_ArrayConstrained\_Vars** | `INT` | `0` | The maximum number of array constrained variables with timelevels that MoL needs to know about (DPRECATED) <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **MoL::MoL\_Num\_ArrayEvolved\_Vars** | `INT` | `0` | The maximum number of array variables to be evolved by MoL (DPRECATED) <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **MoL::MoL\_Num\_ArraySaveAndRestore\_Vars** | `INT` | `0` | The maximum number of array variables to be evolved outside of MoL but that MoL needs to know about (DPRECATED) <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **MoL::MoL\_Num\_Constrained\_Vars** | `INT` | `0` | The maximum number of constrained variables with timelevels that MoL needs to know about (DPRECATED) <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **MoL::MoL\_Num\_Evolved\_Vars** | `INT` | `0` | The maximum number of variables to be evolved by MoL (DPRECATED) <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **MoL::MoL\_Num\_Evolved\_Vars\_Slow** | `INT` | `0` | The maximum number of 'slow' variables to be evolved by MoL (DPRECATED) <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **MoL::MoL\_Num\_SaveAndRestore\_Vars** | `INT` | `0` | The maximum number of variables to be evolved outside of MoL but that MoL needs to know about (DPRECATED) <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **MoL::MoL\_Num\_Scratch\_Levels** | `INT` | `0` | Number of scratch levels required by the ODE method <br> *Tartomány:* `0:*` |
| **MoL::AB\_Type** | `KEYWORD` | `1` | If using the the AB method, which sort <br> *Tartomány:* `fifth order` |
| **MoL::AB\_initially\_reduce\_order** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Reduce order of accuracy initially so that no past timelevels of initial data are required <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **MoL::Generic\_Method\_Descriptor** | `STRING` | `GenericIntermediateSteps = 2    GenericAlphaCoeffs = { 1.0 0.0 0.5 0.5 }    GenericBetaCoeffs = { 1.0 0.5 }` | A string used to create a table containing the description of the generic method <br> *Tartomány:* `.*` |
| **MoL::Generic\_Type** | `KEYWORD` | `RK` | If using the generic method, which sort <br> *Tartomány:* `Efficient RK3 - classical version` |
| **MoL::ICN\_avg\_swapped** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use swapped averages in ICN method? <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **MoL::ICN\_avg\_theta** | `REAL` | `0.5` | theta of averaged ICN method, usually 0.5 <br> *Tartomány:* `0:1` |
| **MoL::MoL\_Intermediate\_Steps** | `INT` | `3` | Number of intermediate steps taken by the ODE method <br> *Tartomány:* `1:*` |
| **MoL::MoL\_Memory\_Always\_On** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Do we keep the scratch arrays allocated all the time? <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **MoL::MoL\_NaN\_Check** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Should the RHS GFs be checked for NaNs? <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **MoL::MoL\_Tiny** | `REAL` | `1.e-15` | Effective local machine zero; required by generic solvers <br> *Tartomány:* `N/A` |
| **MoL::ODE\_Method** | `KEYWORD` | `ICN` | The ODE method use by MoL to do time integration <br> *Tartomány:* `RK4 as fast method and RK2 as slow method` |
| **MoL::RHS\_error\_weight** | `REAL` | `1.0` | Weight of the RHS in the relative error calculation <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:*` |
| **MoL::adaptive\_stepsize** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Choose the time step size adaptively <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **MoL::copy\_ID\_after\_MoL\_PostStep** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | if  initial\_data\_is\_crap  is true, *when* should we copy the		   current time level to all previous time levels:			   false ==> copy *before* MoL\_PostStep  (default, matches old behavior)   true  ==> copy *after*  MoL\_PostStep  (maybe preferable for new code) <br> *Tartomány:* `if  initial\_data\_is\_crap  is true, *when* should we copy the		   current time level to all previous time levels:			   false ==> copy *before* MoL\_PostStep  (default, matches old behavior)   true  ==> copy *after*  MoL\_PostStep  (maybe preferable for new code)` |
| **MoL::disable\_prolongation** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | If Mesh refinement is enabled should we use buffer zones in intermediate steps? <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **MoL::init\_RHS\_zero** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Initialise the RHS to zero <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **MoL::initial\_data\_is\_crap** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | If the initial data routine fails to set up the previous time levels, copy the current backwards <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **MoL::maximum\_absolute\_error** | `REAL` | `1.0e-6` | Maximum allowed absolute error for adaptive stepsize control <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:*)` |
| **MoL::maximum\_decrease** | `REAL` | `10.0` | Maximum stepsize decrease factor <br> *Tartomány:* `(1.0:*)` |
| **MoL::maximum\_increase** | `REAL` | `5.0` | Maximum stepsize increase factor <br> *Tartomány:* `(1.0:*)` |
| **MoL::maximum\_relative\_error** | `REAL` | `1.0e-6` | Maximum allowed relative error for adaptive stepsize control <br> *Tartomány:* `0.0:*)` |
| **MoL::run\_MoL\_PostStep\_in\_Post\_Recover\_Variables** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Schedule the PostStep parts after recovery so that symmetries are automatically done correctly. <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **MoL::safety\_factor** | `REAL` | `0.9` | Safety factor for stepsize control <br> *Tartomány:* `(0.0:*)` |
| **MoL::set\_ID\_boundaries** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Should boundaries be overwritten (via synchronization, prolongation, boundary conditions) by MoL? <br> *Tartomány:* `yes` |
| **MoL::skip\_initial\_copy** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Skip initial copy from previous to current time level <br> *Tartomány:* `no` |
| **MoL::verbose** | `KEYWORD` | `normal` | How verbose should MoL be? <br> *Tartomány:* `Everything you never wanted to know` |

## 📦 Thorn: Multipole<a id='multipole'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
